## [2026-09-22 23:28 UTC] Dashboard tick: Embedded ROI calculators ship on 6 playbook detail pages — operator gets the calculator right where they read the runbook (interactive-tool + cross-page-intelligence)

- **What shipped:** The matching interactive calculator now renders inline at the top of 6 playbook detail pages, between the `Mark as shipped` toggle and the `Goal` meta card. Specifically: (1) `/playbooks/01-abandoned-cart-flow-klaviyo` → `AbandonedCartROICalculator` (3-email + 2-SMS Klaviyo + Postscript forecast); (2) `/playbooks/02-post-purchase-upsell-reconvert` → `PostPurchaseUpsellROICalculator`; (3) `/playbooks/03-checkout-audit-baymard` → `CheckoutAudit` (24-guideline Baymard scorer); (4) `/playbooks/04-welcome-series-klaviyo` → `WelcomeSeriesROICalculator`; (5) `/playbooks/09.5-pdp-ab-testing-program` → `PdpAbTestCalculator` (lift / sample-size / runtime); (6) `/playbooks/10-ai-ad-creative-iteration` → `AiAdCreativeROICalculator`. Each calculator renders with a small `Calculator` accent badge + a one-line description of what it does for that specific playbook. The other 24 playbooks render unchanged — the calculator only appears on playbooks that have a matching tool wired into `/playbooks` or `/cro`.
- **Why this matters:** Before this tick, an operator who clicked into `/playbooks/01-abandoned-cart-flow-klaviyo` (or any of the 5 other playbooks) had to (a) read the runbook, (b) bounce back to `/playbooks` or `/cro` to find the calculator, (c) re-enter their numbers. The calculators already existed and were already wired into `/playbooks` (abandoned-cart, post-purchase, welcome, AI-creative, PDP-A/B) and `/cro` (checkout-audit) — they were just orphaned from the natural reading path. Now the calculator is the first thing the operator sees after `Mark as shipped` and the Goal meta, exactly where the runbook tells them "forecast your numbers before turning this flow on." Each calculator reads its own localStorage key for persistence AND merges the operator's AOV / monthly orders / gross margin from the `ecom-ops:your-store:v1` key on mount — so the same numbers typed on Overview propagate here without re-entry. The math is byte-identical to the Python CLI scripts (`scripts/abandoned_cart_roi.py`, `scripts/checkout_audit_score.py`, `scripts/welcome_series_roi.py`, `scripts/post_purchase_upsell_roi.py`, `scripts/ai_ad_creative_roi.py`, `scripts/pdp_ab_test.py`) — no drift between dashboard and CLI.
- **What file(s) changed:**
  - `dashboard/app/playbooks/[slug]/page.tsx` (+~70 LOC) — added 6 imports (`AbandonedCartROICalculator`, `PostPurchaseUpsellROICalculator`, `WelcomeSeriesROICalculator`, `PdpAbTestCalculator`, `AiAdCreativeROICalculator`, `CheckoutAudit`), a `CALCULATORS` slug → `{ node, label }` registry mapping the 6 slugs to their matching component, a small `<PlaybookCalculator slug={slug} />` client wrapper that renders a `Calculator` accent badge + one-line description + the embedded tool (returns null if no entry matches), and inserted the `<PlaybookCalculator />` call between `<PlaybookShippedToggle />` and the Goal meta card. No other routes touched.
- **Where to see it:**
  - https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/playbooks/01-abandoned-cart-flow-klaviyo (the abandoned-cart playbook — Calculator badge + 3-email + 2-SMS forecast tool inline)
  - https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/playbooks/03-checkout-audit-baymard (the Baymard checklist — Calculator badge + 24-guideline scorer inline)
  - https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/playbooks/04-welcome-series-klaviyo (welcome series — Calculator badge + 4-email forecast)
  - https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/playbooks/09.5-pdp-ab-testing-program (PDP A/B — Calculator badge + sample-size + runtime)
  - https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/playbooks/10-ai-ad-creative-iteration (AI creative — Calculator badge + lift + ROI)
  - https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/playbooks/02-post-purchase-upsell-reconvert (post-purchase upsell — Calculator badge + monthly projection)
  - https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/playbooks/05-migrate-to-klaviyo-postscript (control case — no Calculator badge, no regression)

## [2026-09-22 11:08 UTC] Dashboard-improver tick: Skills Progress Dashboard on /skills — coverage % + confidence tier breakdown + P0/P1/P2/P3 mix + 7-day study pace + recently-studied list + JSON export + reset (state-persistence + cross-page-intelligence + one-click-action).

- **Why:** The dashboard now has 278 skills in the library but the operator had no way to see their coverage. The per-skill "Mark studied" toggle on /skills existed, the detail-page toggle existed, the /today NextSkillToStudy recommendation existed — but the operator couldn't answer "how much of the library have I actually internalized?" without scrolling through 278 rows.
- **What shipped:** `dashboard/src/components/skills-progress-dashboard.tsx` (new, ~21KB) — mounted on `/skills` between NextSkillToStudy and SkillsSearch. Reads `ecom-ops:skills:studied:v1` (the same key 4 other components write to), renders: (a) headline KPIs (coverage, studied/applied/shipped counts), (b) coverage bar with tier-tone (starter 0-24% / rolling 25-49% / scaling 50-74% / complete 75%+ — reuses `progressTierFor()`), (c) per-priority coverage grid (P0/P1/P2/P3 studied-vs-total with bars), (d) 7-day study pace (colored bar dots, hover tooltips with ISO date + count), (e) last-5 recently-studied skill links back to detail pages, (f) one-click JSON export of the full studied map (`skills-progress-YYYY-MM-DD.json`), (g) one-click Markdown summary copy via existing CopyButton, (h) two-tap reset with confirm.
- **Wired:** Same-tab `CustomEvent("ecom-ops:skills:studied-changed")` now dispatched by SkillsSearch `onMark`/`onClear` AND SkillDetailToggle `markStudied`/`updateConfidence`/`clearStudied` — was missing before, so this tick ALSO closes the same-tab sync gap. Cross-tab `storage` event already worked. Hydration-safe (renders deterministic stub before mount to avoid SSR mismatch — same pattern as SkillDetailToggle).
- **Files changed:** +`dashboard/src/components/skills-progress-dashboard.tsx`, M`dashboard/app/skills/page.tsx` (mount), M`dashboard/src/components/skills-search.tsx` (event dispatch), M`dashboard/src/components/skill-detail-toggle.tsx` (event dispatch).
- **Where to see it:** https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/skills — scroll down past the 278-stat row, past "Next skill to study", and the new "Your skill progress" card appears with 4 KPI tiles + coverage bar + 4 priority tiles + 7-day pace chart + recently-studied list + Export/Copy/Reset action row.
- **Build verification:** `cd dashboard && NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` SUCCEEDED on first try, /skills route 9.38 kB (no size regression), no type errors, no lint warnings.
- **Deploy verification:** `vercel deploy --prod --yes --token <vcp>` SUCCEEDED → `dashboard-qwijxaeg8-mattiasadem-5021s-projects.vercel.app`, deployment-id `dpl_EpdxMbdTPGv2W9eRTzQVo118W2uv`, `READY`, `target: production`. `vercel alias set` rotated canonical alias in 995ms. `curl https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/skills | grep "Your skill progress"` confirmed SSR contains the new component. Bundle `/_next/static/chunks/app/skills/page-fcd4e0045300c87b.js` contains the new component code.
- **Next action:** Natural follow-up: per-category progress breakdown (retention/acquisition/conversion/etc. coverage % side-by-side) — would let the operator see "I have 0% of fulfillment skills covered" instantly. Could also add a "study streak" badge (consecutive days with ≥1 mark) on the dashboard header.

## [2026-09-21 16:43 UTC] Dashboard tick: Move #6.10 attribution-health-alert calculator gains Your-store personalised recovery projection

- **Feature class:** cross-page-intelligence
- **What shipped:** `dashboard/src/components/attribution-health-alert-calculator.tsx` now renders a new **Personalised recovery · tied to Your-store** panel directly above the Path A/B/C/D/E recommendation strip on `/attribution-health-alert-archive`. The panel reads AOV / monthly orders / gross margin from the canonical `ecom-ops:your-store:v1` localStorage key on mount (with cross-tab `storage` event sync), then projects the picked Path's `year1IncrementalAttributionRecovery` band onto the operator's actual revenue base: annual gross revenue · Y1 net recovery (after subtractive infra cost) · recovery as a % of annual revenue · net margin $ + infra payback months. Live-numbers badge lights up when Your-store is set and annualised revenue >= $12k. Falls back gracefully to canonical $75 x 1,000 x 70% defaults with a clear "Industry-median default" badge and CTA to set Your-store on Overview.
- **Why it matters:** the calculator's existing `year1IncrementalAttributionRecoveryLow`/`High` bands are flat dollar figures (Path B = $5k-$15k, Path C = $30k-$200k, Path D = $200k-$1.6M) — they don't scale with the operator's actual revenue. For a $5M-yr brand, a $15k Path B recovery is 0.3% of annual revenue; for a $50M-yr brand the same $15k is 0.03%. The personalised panel re-frames that as `% of annual revenue` + `infra payback months` so the operator can answer the canonical pre-install question "is this alert infra worth it for my size of business?" with their actual numbers.
- **Math:** `annualised_revenue = aov x monthly_orders x 12`; `net_recovery = recovery - cost_mid`; `recovery_pct = net_recovery / annualised_revenue x 100`; `payback_months = cost_mid / (recovery_mid / 12)`; `margin_recovery = net_recovery x gross_margin`. All canonical `PATH_*` constants and `projectPerPathRecovery()` outputs from `lib/attribution-health-alert.ts` are reused — no math drift from the Python CLI (which still passes all 112 tests).
- **Files:** `dashboard/src/components/attribution-health-alert-calculator.tsx` (+213 lines — added `loadYourStore` import + hydration effect + cross-tab `storage` listener + `<PersonalizedRecoveryPanel>` sub-component + insertion into the outputs block).
- **Where to see it:** https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/attribution-health-alert-archive — scroll past the calculator header + 4 input grids; the new personalised panel is the first card in the outputs block. Set Your-store on https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/ to see the "Live numbers" badge light up and the $ projections re-frame against your actual AOV x orders x margin.
- **Deploy status:** **DEPLOY SUCCEEDED.** Live `https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/attribution-health-alert-archive` returns HTTP 200 with the canonical 5-path recommendation strip AND the new personalised recovery panel (verified via curl + grep — `<div id="attribution-health-alert-personalized">` renders with default-fallback message since SSR has no localStorage access; the panel hydrates client-side). Vercel alias rotated in **938ms** from `dashboard-3m62q0893-mattiasadem-5021s-projects.vercel.app` to canonical `ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app`. Next.js build produced 297 static pages, no type errors, no lint warnings.
- **Next action:** natural follow-up — wire the same `Your-store` personalised panel into the 5 remaining calculators that don't yet read `ecom-ops:your-store:v1`: `attribution-quality-audit.tsx` (Gate F cohort roundtrip + Gate G drift both have $/order cost implications), `lifecycle-flow-health-audit.tsx` (revenue-per-1k is exactly the Your-store projection), `generative-ai-engine-calculator.tsx` (Year-1 content-output-value scales with operator margin), `snap-pinterest-attribution-audit.tsx`, and `tiktok-attribution-audit.tsx`. Each follows the same 5-step wiring recipe (load on mount + storage listener + read from `Your-store` + project a personalised $ band + show "Live numbers" badge).

## [2026-09-21 23:55 UTC] Dashboard tick: /playbooks/[slug] detail route ships — 30 playbook pages now click-through (cross-page-intelligence + state-persistence + interactive-tool)

- **What shipped:** A real detail route at `/playbooks/[slug]` that renders each of the 30 playbooks (e.g. `01-abandoned-cart-flow-klaviyo`, `03-checkout-audit-baymard`, `30-quantcast-competitor-creative-monitoring`) as a fully styled page with H1 + freshness + section badges + the Goal lead-block bullets + the full markdown body rendered to HTML (H2/H3 with scroll anchors, bullet/numbered lists, tables, blockquotes, code fences, inline bold/italic/code/links). On the index `/playbooks`, the playbook card title AND the `/playbooks/{file}` slug line are now real `next/link` → click-through to the detail page. Each detail page has a `Mark as shipped` toggle that writes to the same `ecom-ops:shipped-playbooks:v1` localStorage key the index tracker and the `RecentlyShippedPlaybooksCard` on `/` already read — flipping the toggle on the detail page instantly updates the badge on the index AND the progress card on Overview. Notes field surfaces the operator's one-line outcome back on the index tracker.
- **Why this matters:** Before this tick, `/playbooks` was a search-and-filter index with NO click-through — operators could see the Goal bullets + section preview, but to actually read the runbook they had to scroll inside `<details>` or leave the dashboard for the raw markdown. Now each card is one click from a full SSR-rendered playbook page with `force-static` HTML output. The shipped toggle was the #1 missing piece of cross-page intelligence: the index tracker wrote the state, but there was no first-class place to flip it from.
- **What file(s) changed:**
  - `dashboard/app/playbooks/[slug]/page.tsx` (NEW, 11.5kB) — detail route, 30 prerendered paths via `generateStaticParams` over `content.playbooks[]`; reads raw markdown from `src/playbooks/{file}.md` (build-time copy), falls back to `/data/workspace/ecommerce-ops/playbooks/{file}.md` for local dev.
  - `dashboard/src/components/playbook-shipped-toggle.tsx` (NEW, 4.7kB) — client component; `useEffect` hydrate from `loadShippedPlaybooks()` on mount, persist via `saveShippedPlaybooks(shipped)` on change, emit `ecom-ops:shipped-playbooks:update` custom event for same-tab cross-component sync.
  - `dashboard/src/components/playbook-search.tsx` — wrap the card title (`<CardTitle>`) and the `/playbooks/{file}` CardDescription in `next/link` to the new route; added `import Link from "next/link"`.
  - `dashboard/scripts/parse-content.mjs` — new `syncPlaybookCopies(sourceFiles)` helper (rm + mkdir + writeFile) called after `parsePlaybooks()`; mirrors the `src/skills/` build-time copy pattern so the detail route can read raw markdown at runtime without depending on the workspace path. Now `parse-content.mjs` writes `src/playbooks/*.md` (30 files) on every run.
  - `dashboard/src/playbooks/*.md` (NEW, 30 files, ~1.4 MB total) — build-time copies of the source playbooks, refreshed every parse run.
